      "path": "javaparser-core-testing-bdd/src/test/java/com/github/javaparser/steps/SharedSteps.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2007-2010 Júlio Vilmar Gesser.\n * Copyright (C) 2011, 2013-2019 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.steps;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.CompilationUnit;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.BodyDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.MethodDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.TypeDeclaration;\nimport org.hamcrest.CoreMatchers;\nimport org.jbehave.core.annotations.Given;\nimport org.jbehave.core.annotations.Then;\nimport org.jbehave.core.annotations.When;\n\nimport java.io.File;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.net.URISyntaxException;\nimport java.net.URL;\nimport java.util.Map;\n\nimport static com.github.javaparser.StaticJavaParser.parse;\nimport static org.hamcrest.core.Is.is;\nimport static org.hamcrest.core.IsEqual.equalTo;\nimport static org.hamcrest.core.IsNot.not;\nimport static org.hamcrest.text.IsEqualCompressingWhiteSpace.equalToCompressingWhiteSpace;\nimport static org.hamcrest.MatcherAssert.assertThat;\n\npublic class SharedSteps {\n\n    /* Map that maintains shares state across step classes.  If manipulating the objects in the map you must update the state */\n    private Map<String, Object> state;\n\n    public SharedSteps(Map<String, Object> state) {\n        this.state = state;\n    }\n\n    /*\n     * Given steps\n     */\n\n    @Given(\"a CompilationUnit\")\n    public void givenACompilationUnit() {\n        state.put(\"cu1\", new CompilationUnit());\n    }\n\n    @Given(\"a second CompilationUnit\")\n    public void givenASecondCompilationUnit() {\n        state.put(\"cu2\", new CompilationUnit());\n    }\n\n    /*\n     * When steps\n     */\n\n    @When(\"the following source is parsed:$classSrc\")\n    public void whenTheFollowingSourceIsParsed(String classSrc) {\n        state.put(\"cu1\", parse(classSrc.trim()));\n    }\n\n    @When(\"the following source is parsed (trimming space):$classSrc\")\n    public void whenTheFollowingSourceIsParsedTrimmingSpace(String classSrc) {\n        state.put(\"cu1\", parse(classSrc.trim()));\n    }\n\n    @When(\"the following sources is parsed by the second CompilationUnit:$classSrc\")\n    public void whenTheFollowingSourcesIsParsedBytTheSecondCompilationUnit(String classSrc) {\n        state.put(\"cu2\", parse(classSrc.trim()));\n    }\n\n    @When(\"file \\\"$fileName\\\" is parsed\")\n    public void whenTheJavaFileIsParsed(String fileName) throws IOException, URISyntaxException {\n        URL url = getClass().getResource(\"../samples/\" + fileName);\n        CompilationUnit compilationUnit = parse(new File(url.toURI()));\n        state.put(\"cu1\", compilationUnit);\n    }\n\n    @Then(\"the CompilationUnit is equal to the second CompilationUnit\")\n    public void thenTheCompilationUnitIsEqualToTheSecondCompilationUnit() {\n        CompilationUnit compilationUnit = (CompilationUnit) state.get(\"cu1\");\n        CompilationUnit compilationUnit2 = (CompilationUnit) state.get(\"cu2\");\n\n        assertThat(compilationUnit, is(equalTo(compilationUnit2)));\n    }\n\n    @Then(\"the CompilationUnit has the same hashcode to the second CompilationUnit\")\n    public void thenTheCompilationUnitHasTheSameHashcodeToTheSecondCompilationUnit() {\n        CompilationUnit compilationUnit = (CompilationUnit) state.get(\"cu1\");\n        CompilationUnit compilationUnit2 = (CompilationUnit) state.get(\"cu2\");\n\n        assertThat(compilationUnit.hashCode(), is(equalTo(compilationUnit2.hashCode())));\n    }\n\n    @Then(\"the CompilationUnit is not equal to the second CompilationUnit\")\n    public void thenTheCompilationUnitIsNotEqualToTheSecondCompilationUnit() {\n        CompilationUnit compilationUnit = (CompilationUnit) state.get(\"cu1\");\n        CompilationUnit compilationUnit2 = (CompilationUnit) state.get(\"cu2\");\n\n        assertThat(compilationUnit, not(equalTo(compilationUnit2)));\n    }\n\n    @Then(\"the CompilationUnit has a different hashcode to the second CompilationUnit\")\n    public void thenTheCompilationUnitHasADifferentHashcodeToTheSecondCompilationUnit() {\n        CompilationUnit compilationUnit = (CompilationUnit) state.get(\"cu1\");\n        CompilationUnit compilationUnit2 = (CompilationUnit) state.get(\"cu2\");\n\n        assertThat(compilationUnit.hashCode(), not(equalTo(compilationUnit2.hashCode())));\n    }\n\n    @Then(\"the expected source should be:$classSrc\")\n    public void thenTheExpectedSourcesShouldBe(String classSrc) {\n        CompilationUnit compilationUnit = (CompilationUnit) state.get(\"cu1\");\n        assertThat(compilationUnit.toString(), CoreMatchers.is(equalToCompressingWhiteSpace(classSrc)));\n    }\n\n    public static <T extends BodyDeclaration<?>> T getMemberByTypeAndPosition(TypeDeclaration<?> typeDeclaration, int position, Class<T> typeClass) {\n        int typeCount = 0;\n        for (BodyDeclaration<?> declaration : typeDeclaration.getMembers()) {\n            if (declaration.getClass().equals(typeClass)) {\n                if (typeCount == position) {\n                    return (T) declaration;\n                }\n                typeCount++;\n            }\n        }\n        throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"No member \" + typeClass + \" at position: \" + position);\n    }\n\n    public static MethodDeclaration getMethodByPositionAndClassPosition(CompilationUnit compilationUnit,\n                                                                        int methodPosition, int classPosition) {\n        TypeDeclaration<?> type = compilationUnit.getType(classPosition - 1);\n\n        int memberCount = 0;\n        int methodCount = 0;\n        for (BodyDeclaration<?> bodyDeclaration : type.getMembers()) {\n            if (bodyDeclaration instanceof MethodDeclaration) {\n                if (methodCount == methodPosition - 1) {\n                    return (MethodDeclaration) type.getMember(memberCount);\n                }\n                methodCount++;\n            }\n            memberCount++;\n        }\n        throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"Method not found at position \" + methodPosition + \"in class \" + classPosition);\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "javaparser-core-testing/src/test/java/com/github/javaparser/LineSeparatorProcessorTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package com.github.javaparser;\n\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\nimport java.util.Optional;\n\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.CompilationUnit;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.Modifier;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.Node;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.NodeList;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.ClassOrInterfaceDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.FieldDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.VariableDeclarator;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.type.ClassOrInterfaceType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.printer.lexicalpreservation.AbstractLexicalPreservingTest;\nimport com.github.javaparser.printer.lexicalpreservation.LexicalPreservingPrinter;\nimport com.github.javaparser.utils.LineSeparator;\n\npublic class LineSeparatorProcessorTest extends AbstractLexicalPreservingTest{\n\n    // TODO: Add more tests outside the \"happy path\" (e.g. mixed EOL, no EOL, etc.)\n\n    /*\n     * This test case must prevent an UnsupportedOperation Removed throwed by LexicalPreservation when we try to replace an expression\n     */\n    public void doTest(LineSeparator lineSeparator) {\n        String eol = lineSeparator.asRawString();\n\n        considerCode(\"\" +\n                \"    public class Foo { //comment\" + eol +\n                \"        private String a;\" + eol +\n                \"        private String b;\" + eol +\n                \"        private String c;\" + eol +\n                \"        private String d;\" + eol +\n                \"    }\");\n\n        // Note: Expect the platform's EOL character when printing\n        String expected = \"\" +\n                \"    public class Foo { //comment\" + eol +\n                \"    private String newField;\" + eol +\n                \"    \" + eol +\n                \"    private String a;\" + eol +\n                \"        private String b;\" + eol +\n                \"        private String c;\" + eol +\n                \"        private String d;\" + eol +\n                \"    }\";\n\n\n        // create a new field declaration\n        VariableDeclarator variable = new VariableDeclarator(new ClassOrInterfaceType(\"String\"), \"newField\");\n        FieldDeclaration fd = new FieldDeclaration(new NodeList(Modifier.privateModifier()), variable);\n        Optional<ClassOrInterfaceDeclaration> cd = cu.findFirst(ClassOrInterfaceDeclaration.class);\n\n        // add the new variable\n        cd.get().getMembers().addFirst(fd);\n\n        // should be printed like this\n//        System.out.println(\"\\n\\nOriginal:\\n\" + original);\n//        System.out.println(\"\\n\\nExpected:\\n\" + expected);\n\n        // but the result is\n        final String actual = LexicalPreservingPrinter.print(cu);\n//        System.out.println(\"\\n\\nActual:\\n\" + actual);\n\n\n        // The LineEndingProcessingProvider sets the line ending to the root node.\n        // Child nodes should then \"inherit\" then line ending style.\n        LineSeparator lineSeparator_cu = cu.getLineEndingStyle();\n        LineSeparator lineSeparator_fd = fd.getLineEndingStyle();\n\n//        System.out.println(\"lineSeparator_cu.describe() = \" + lineSeparator_cu.describe());\n//        System.out.println(\"lineSeparator_fd.describe() = \" + lineSeparator_fd.describe());\n\n        // Assert that it has been detected and injected correctly.\n        LineSeparator detectedLineSeparator = LineSeparator.detect(actual);\n        assertEquals(lineSeparator, detectedLineSeparator);\n        assertEquals(lineSeparator, lineSeparator_cu);\n        assertEquals(lineSeparator, lineSeparator_fd);\n\n        // The line ending data is injected at the root node, thus should only exist there.\n        assertTrue(cu.containsData(Node.LINE_SEPARATOR_KEY), \"Expected the processor provider to have set the data on the root node.\");\n        assertFalse(fd.containsData(Node.LINE_SEPARATOR_KEY), \"Expected the line ending value to have been inherited, not set directly\");\n\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    public void testWithCr() {\n        doTest(LineSeparator.CR);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    public void testWithCrLf() {\n        doTest(LineSeparator.CRLF);\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    public void testWithLf() {\n        doTest(LineSeparator.LF);\n    }\n\n\n    // TODO: Test for textblocks\n\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "javaparser-core-testing/src/test/java/com/github/javaparser/ast/stmt/TryStmtTest.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2007-2010 Júlio Vilmar Gesser.\n * Copyright (C) 2011, 2013-2019 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.ast.stmt;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.JavaParser;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ParseResult;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ParseStart;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ParserConfiguration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.FieldAccessExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.NameExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.VariableDeclarationExpr;\nimport org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;\n\nimport static com.github.javaparser.ParserConfiguration.LanguageLevel.*;\nimport static com.github.javaparser.Providers.provider;\nimport static com.github.javaparser.utils.TestUtils.assertInstanceOf;\nimport static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;\n\nclass TryStmtTest {\n    @Test\n    void simpleTest() {\n        TryStmt tryStmt = parse9(\"try(Reader x = new FileReader()){}\");\n        assertInstanceOf(VariableDeclarationExpr.class, tryStmt.getResources().get(0));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void multipleTest() {\n        TryStmt tryStmt = parse9(\"try(Reader x = new FileReader(); Reader x = new FileReader()){}\");\n        assertInstanceOf(VariableDeclarationExpr.class, tryStmt.getResources().get(0));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void modifiersTest() {\n        TryStmt tryStmt = parse9(\"try(final @A Reader x = new FileReader()){}\");\n        assertInstanceOf(VariableDeclarationExpr.class, tryStmt.getResources().get(0));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void simpleVariable() {\n        TryStmt tryStmt = parse9(\"try(a){}\");\n        assertInstanceOf(NameExpr.class, tryStmt.getResources().get(0));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void twoSimpleVariables() {\n        TryStmt tryStmt = parse9(\"try(a;b){}\");\n        assertInstanceOf(NameExpr.class, tryStmt.getResources().get(0));\n        assertInstanceOf(NameExpr.class, tryStmt.getResources().get(1));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void complexVariable() {\n        TryStmt tryStmt = parse9(\"try(a.b.c){}\");\n        assertInstanceOf(FieldAccessExpr.class, tryStmt.getResources().get(0));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void superAccess() {\n        TryStmt tryStmt = parse9(\"try(super.a){}\");\n        assertInstanceOf(FieldAccessExpr.class, tryStmt.getResources().get(0));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void outerClassAccess() {\n        TryStmt tryStmt = parse9(\"try(X.this.a){}\");\n        assertInstanceOf(FieldAccessExpr.class, tryStmt.getResources().get(0));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void varTestJava10() {\n        TryStmt tryStmt = parse10(\"try(var x = new FileReader()){}\");\n        assertInstanceOf(VariableDeclarationExpr.class, tryStmt.getResources().get(0));\n    }\n\n    @Test\n    void varTestJava11() {\n        TryStmt tryStmt = parse11(\"try(var x = new FileReader()){}\");\n        assertInstanceOf(VariableDeclarationExpr.class, tryStmt.getResources().get(0));\n    }\n\n    private <T> T parse9(String code) {\n        JavaParser parser = new JavaParser(new ParserConfiguration().setLanguageLevel(JAVA_9));\n        ParseResult<Statement> result = parser.parse(ParseStart.STATEMENT, provider(code));\n        assertTrue(result.isSuccessful(), result.toString());\n        return (T) result.getResult().get();\n    }\n\n    private <T> T parse10(String code) {\n        JavaParser parser = new JavaParser(new ParserConfiguration().setLanguageLevel(JAVA_10));\n        ParseResult<Statement> result = parser.parse(ParseStart.STATEMENT, provider(code));\n        assertTrue(result.isSuccessful(), result.toString());\n        return (T) result.getResult().get();\n    }\n\n    private <T> T parse11(String code) {\n        JavaParser parser = new JavaParser(new ParserConfiguration().setLanguageLevel(JAVA_11));\n        ParseResult<Statement> result = parser.parse(ParseStart.STATEMENT, provider(code));\n        assertTrue(result.isSuccessful(), result.toString());\n        return (T) result.getResult().get();\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "javaparser-core-testing/src/test/resources/com/github/javaparser/utils/projectroot/issue2615/with_module_info/demo/Main.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "package demo;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ParseResult;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.CompilationUnit;\nimport com.github.javaparser.utils.ParserCollectionStrategy;\nimport com.github.javaparser.utils.ProjectRoot;\nimport com.github.javaparser.utils.SourceRoot;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.nio.file.Path;\nimport java.nio.file.Paths;\n\n/* If there is a module declaration in the root directory, JavaParser doesn't find any .java files. */\npublic class Main {\n\n    public static void main(String[] args) {\n        if (args.length < 1) {\n            System.err.println(\"Usage: provide one or more directory names to process\");\n            System.exit(1);\n        }\n        for (String dir : args) {\n            process(dir);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void process(String dir) {\n        Path root = Paths.get(dir);\n        Callback cb = new Callback();\n        ProjectRoot projectRoot = new ParserCollectionStrategy().collect(root);\n        projectRoot.getSourceRoots().forEach(sourceRoot -> {\n            try {\n                sourceRoot.parse(\"\", cb);\n            } catch (IOException e) {\n                System.err.println(\"IOException: \" + e);\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    private static class Callback implements SourceRoot.Callback {\n\n        @Override\n        public Result process(Path localPath, Path absolutePath, ParseResult<CompilationUnit> result) {\n            System.out.printf(\"Found %s%n\", absolutePath);\n            return Result.SAVE;\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "package demo;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ParseResult;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.CompilationUnit;\nimport com.github.javaparser.utils.ParserCollectionStrategy;\nimport com.github.javaparser.utils.ProjectRoot;\nimport com.github.javaparser.utils.SourceRoot;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.nio.file.Path;\nimport java.nio.file.Paths;\n\n/* If there is a module declaration in the root directory, JavaParser doesn't find any .java files. */\npublic class Main {\n\n    public static void main(String[] args) {\n        if (args.length < 1) {\n            System.err.println(\"Usage: provide one or more directory names to process\");\n            System.exit(1);\n        }\n        for (String dir : args) {\n            process(dir);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void process(String dir) {\n        Path root = Paths.get(dir);\n        Callback cb = new Callback();\n        ProjectRoot projectRoot = new ParserCollectionStrategy().collect(root);\n        projectRoot.getSourceRoots().forEach(sourceRoot -> {\n            try {\n                sourceRoot.parse(\"\", cb);\n            } catch (IOException e) {\n                System.err.println(\"IOException: \" + e);\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    private static class Callback implements SourceRoot.Callback {\n\n        @Override\n        public Result process(Path localPath, Path absolutePath, ParseResult<CompilationUnit> result) {\n            System.out.printf(\"Found %s%n\", absolutePath);\n            return Result.SAVE;\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

      "content": "package demo;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ParseResult;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.CompilationUnit;\nimport com.github.javaparser.utils.ParserCollectionStrategy;\nimport com.github.javaparser.utils.ProjectRoot;\nimport com.github.javaparser.utils.SourceRoot;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.nio.file.Path;\nimport java.nio.file.Paths;\n\n/* If there is a module declaration in the root directory, JavaParser doesn't find any .java files. */\npublic class Main {\n\n    public static void main(String[] args) {\n        if (args.length < 1) {\n            System.err.println(\"Usage: provide one or more directory names to process\");\n            System.exit(1);\n        }\n        for (String dir : args) {\n            process(dir);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void process(String dir) {\n        Path root = Paths.get(dir);\n        Callback cb = new Callback();\n        ProjectRoot projectRoot = new ParserCollectionStrategy().collect(root);\n        projectRoot.getSourceRoots().forEach(sourceRoot -> {\n            try {\n                sourceRoot.parse(\"\", cb);\n            } catch (IOException e) {\n                System.err.println(\"IOException: \" + e);\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    private static class Callback implements SourceRoot.Callback {\n\n        @Override\n        public Result process(Path localPath, Path absolutePath, ParseResult<CompilationUnit> result) {\n            System.out.printf(\"Found %s%n\", absolutePath);\n            return Result.SAVE;\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2007-2010 Júlio Vilmar Gesser.\n * Copyright (C) 2011, 2013-2021 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\npackage com.github.javaparser.printer.configuration;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.printer.PrettyPrinter;\nimport com.github.javaparser.printer.configuration.DefaultPrinterConfiguration.ConfigOption;\nimport com.github.javaparser.printer.configuration.Indentation.IndentType;\n\nimport java.util.Optional;\nimport java.util.Set;\n\nimport static com.github.javaparser.utils.Utils.*;\n\n/**\n * Configuration options for the {@link PrettyPrinter}.\n * This class is no longer acceptable to use because it is not sufficiently configurable and it is too tied to a specific implementation\n * <p> Use {@link PrinterConfiguration interface or DefaultPrinterConfiguration default implementation } instead.\n * @deprecated This class could be removed in a future version. Use default DefaultPrinterConfiguration.\n */\n@Deprecated\npublic class PrettyPrinterConfiguration implements PrinterConfiguration {\n\n    PrinterConfiguration wrappedConfiguration;\n\n    /*\n     * Default constructor\n     */\n    public PrettyPrinterConfiguration() {\n        this.wrappedConfiguration = new DefaultPrinterConfiguration();\n    }\n\n    /*\n     * returns the indentation parameters\n     */\n    public Indentation getIndentation() {\n        return wrappedConfiguration.get(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.INDENTATION)).get().asValue();\n    }\n\n    public PrettyPrinterConfiguration setIndentation(Indentation indentation) {\n        wrappedConfiguration.addOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.INDENTATION, indentation));\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @return the string that will be used to indent.\n     * @deprecated (@see Indentation.getIndent())\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public String getIndent() {\n        return getIndentation().getIndent();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @return the indentation size.\n     * @deprecated (@see Indentation.size)\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public int getIndentSize() {\n        return getIndentation().getSize();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Set the size of the indent in characters.\n     * @deprecated (@see Indentation.size())\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public PrettyPrinterConfiguration setIndentSize(int indentSize) {\n        Indentation indentation = getIndentation().setSize(assertNonNegative(indentSize));\n        setIndentation(indentation);\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get the type of indent to produce.\n     * @deprecated (@see Indentation.type)\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public IndentType getIndentType() {\n        return getIndentation().getType();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Set the type of indent to produce.\n     * @deprecated (@see Indentation.type())\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public PrettyPrinterConfiguration setIndentType(IndentType indentType) {\n        Indentation indentation = getIndentation().setType(assertNotNull(indentType));\n        setIndentation(indentation);\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Get the tab width for pretty aligning.\n     * @deprecated (@see Indentation.size)\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public int getTabWidth() {\n        return getIndentation().getSize();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Set the tab width for pretty aligning.\n     * @deprecated (@see Indentation.size)\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public PrettyPrinterConfiguration setTabWidth(int tabWidth) {\n        setIndentSize(assertPositive(tabWidth));\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    public boolean isOrderImports() {\n        return wrappedConfiguration.get(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.ORDER_IMPORTS)).isPresent();\n    }\n\n    public boolean isPrintComments() {\n        return wrappedConfiguration.get(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.PRINT_COMMENTS)).isPresent();\n    }\n\n    public boolean isIgnoreComments() {\n        return !wrappedConfiguration.get(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.PRINT_COMMENTS)).isPresent();\n    }\n\n    public boolean isSpaceAroundOperators() {\n        return wrappedConfiguration.get(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.SPACE_AROUND_OPERATORS)).isPresent();\n    }\n\n    public boolean isPrintJavadoc() {\n        return wrappedConfiguration.get(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.PRINT_JAVADOC)).isPresent();\n    }\n\n    public boolean isColumnAlignParameters() {\n        return wrappedConfiguration.get(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.COLUMN_ALIGN_PARAMETERS)).isPresent();\n    }\n\n    public boolean isColumnAlignFirstMethodChain() {\n        return wrappedConfiguration.get(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.COLUMN_ALIGN_FIRST_METHOD_CHAIN)).isPresent();\n    }\n\n    public boolean isIndentCaseInSwitch() {\n        return wrappedConfiguration.get(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.INDENT_CASE_IN_SWITCH)).isPresent();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * When true, all comments will be printed, unless printJavadoc is false, then only line and block comments will be\n     * printed.\n     */\n    public PrettyPrinterConfiguration setPrintComments(boolean printComments) {\n        wrappedConfiguration = printComments ? addOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.PRINT_COMMENTS)) : removeOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.PRINT_COMMENTS));\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * When true, Javadoc will be printed.\n     */\n    public PrettyPrinterConfiguration setPrintJavadoc(boolean printJavadoc) {\n        wrappedConfiguration = printJavadoc ? addOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.PRINT_JAVADOC)) : removeOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.PRINT_JAVADOC));\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Set if there should be spaces between operators\n     */\n    public PrettyPrinterConfiguration setSpaceAroundOperators(boolean spaceAroundOperators) {\n        wrappedConfiguration = spaceAroundOperators ? addOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.SPACE_AROUND_OPERATORS)) : removeOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.SPACE_AROUND_OPERATORS));\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    public PrettyPrinterConfiguration setColumnAlignParameters(boolean columnAlignParameters) {\n        wrappedConfiguration = columnAlignParameters ? addOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.COLUMN_ALIGN_PARAMETERS)) : removeOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.COLUMN_ALIGN_PARAMETERS));\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    public PrettyPrinterConfiguration setColumnAlignFirstMethodChain(boolean columnAlignFirstMethodChain) {\n        wrappedConfiguration = columnAlignFirstMethodChain ? addOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.COLUMN_ALIGN_FIRST_METHOD_CHAIN)) : removeOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.COLUMN_ALIGN_FIRST_METHOD_CHAIN));\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    public PrettyPrinterConfiguration setIndentCaseInSwitch(boolean indentInSwitch) {\n        wrappedConfiguration = indentInSwitch ? addOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.INDENT_CASE_IN_SWITCH)) : removeOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.INDENT_CASE_IN_SWITCH));\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    public String getEndOfLineCharacter() {\n        return wrappedConfiguration.get(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.END_OF_LINE_CHARACTER)).get().asValue();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Set the character to append when a line should end. Example values: \"\\n\", \"\\r\\n\", \"\".\n     */\n    public PrettyPrinterConfiguration setEndOfLineCharacter(String endOfLineCharacter) {\n        addOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.END_OF_LINE_CHARACTER, endOfLineCharacter));\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * When true, orders imports by alphabetically.\n     */\n    public PrettyPrinterConfiguration setOrderImports(boolean orderImports) {\n        wrappedConfiguration = orderImports ? addOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.ORDER_IMPORTS)) : removeOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.ORDER_IMPORTS));\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    public int getMaxEnumConstantsToAlignHorizontally() {\n        return wrappedConfiguration.get(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.MAX_ENUM_CONSTANTS_TO_ALIGN_HORIZONTALLY)).get().asInteger();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * By default enum constants get aligned like this:\n     * <pre>\n     *     enum X {\n     *        A, B, C, D\n     *     }\n     * </pre>\n     * until the amount of constants passes this currentValue (5 by default).\n     * Then they get aligned like this:\n     * <pre>\n     *     enum X {\n     *        A,\n     *        B,\n     *        C,\n     *        D,\n     *        E,\n     *        F,\n     *        G\n     *     }\n     * </pre>\n     * Set it to a large number to always align horizontally.\n     * Set it to 1 or less to always align vertically.\n     */\n    public PrettyPrinterConfiguration setMaxEnumConstantsToAlignHorizontally(int maxEnumConstantsToAlignHorizontally) {\n        addOption(new DefaultConfigurationOption(ConfigOption.MAX_ENUM_CONSTANTS_TO_ALIGN_HORIZONTALLY, maxEnumConstantsToAlignHorizontally));\n        return this;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public PrinterConfiguration addOption(ConfigurationOption option) {\n        return wrappedConfiguration.addOption(option);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean isActivated(ConfigurationOption option) {\n        return wrappedConfiguration.isActivated(option);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public Optional<ConfigurationOption> get(ConfigurationOption option) {\n        return wrappedConfiguration.get(option);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public Set<ConfigurationOption> get() {\n        return wrappedConfiguration.get();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public PrinterConfiguration removeOption(ConfigurationOption option) {\n        return wrappedConfiguration.removeOption(option);\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "javaparser-core/src/main/java/com/github/javaparser/printer/lexicalpreservation/changes/ListAdditionChange.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2007-2010 Júlio Vilmar Gesser.\n * Copyright (C) 2011, 2013-2021 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\npackage com.github.javaparser.printer.lexicalpreservation.changes;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.Node;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.NodeList;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.observer.ObservableProperty;\n\nimport java.util.Optional;\n\n/**\n * The Addition of an element to a list.\n */\npublic class ListAdditionChange implements Change {\n\n    private final ObservableProperty observableProperty;\n\n    private final int index;\n\n    private final Node nodeAdded;\n\n    public ListAdditionChange(ObservableProperty observableProperty, int index, Node nodeAdded) {\n        this.observableProperty = observableProperty;\n        this.index = index;\n        this.nodeAdded = nodeAdded;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public Object getValue(ObservableProperty property, Node node) {\n        if (property == observableProperty) {\n            Object currentRawValue = new NoChange().getValue(property, node);\n            if (currentRawValue instanceof Optional) {\n                Optional<?> optional = (Optional<?>) currentRawValue;\n                currentRawValue = optional.orElse(null);\n            }\n            if (!(currentRawValue instanceof NodeList)) {\n                throw new IllegalStateException(\"Expected NodeList, found \" + currentRawValue.getClass().getCanonicalName());\n            }\n            NodeList<Node> currentNodeList = (NodeList<Node>) currentRawValue;\n            // Note: When adding to a node list children get assigned the list's parent, thus we must set the list's parent before adding children (#2592).\n            NodeList<Node> newNodeList = new NodeList<>();\n            newNodeList.setParentNode(currentNodeList.getParentNodeForChildren());\n            newNodeList.addAll(currentNodeList);\n            // Perform modification -- add to the list\n            newNodeList.add(index, nodeAdded);\n            return newNodeList;\n        } else {\n            return new NoChange().getValue(property, node);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "javaparser-symbol-solver-core/src/main/java/com/github/javaparser/symbolsolver/SourceFileInfoExtractor.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2015-2016 Federico Tomassetti\n * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.CompilationUnit;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.ImportDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.Node;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.PackageDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.ClassOrInterfaceDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.MethodDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.VariableDeclarator;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.Expression;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.MethodCallExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.stmt.Statement;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.stmt.SwitchEntry;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.TypeSolver;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedMethodDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedTypeDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.SymbolReference;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedReferenceType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.JavaParserFacade;\n\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.PrintStream;\nimport java.nio.file.FileVisitResult;\nimport java.nio.file.Files;\nimport java.nio.file.Path;\nimport java.nio.file.SimpleFileVisitor;\nimport java.nio.file.attribute.BasicFileAttributes;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport static com.github.javaparser.StaticJavaParser.parse;\nimport static com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparser.Navigator.demandParentNode;\nimport static java.util.Comparator.comparing;\n\n/**\n * Resolves resolvable nodes from one or more source files, and reports the results.\n * It is mainly intended as an example usage of JavaSymbolSolver.\n *\n * @author Federico Tomassetti\n */\npublic class SourceFileInfoExtractor {\n\n    private final TypeSolver typeSolver;\n\n    private int successes = 0;\n    private int failures = 0;\n    private int unsupported = 0;\n    private boolean printFileName = true;\n    private PrintStream out = System.out;\n    private PrintStream err = System.err;\n    private boolean verbose = false;\n\n    public SourceFileInfoExtractor(TypeSolver typeSolver) {\n        this.typeSolver = typeSolver;\n    }\n\n    public void setVerbose(boolean verbose) {\n        this.verbose = verbose;\n    }\n\n    public void setPrintFileName(boolean printFileName) {\n        this.printFileName = printFileName;\n    }\n\n    public void setOut(PrintStream out) {\n        this.out = out;\n    }\n\n    public void setErr(PrintStream err) {\n        this.err = err;\n    }\n\n    public int getSuccesses() {\n        return successes;\n    }\n\n    public int getUnsupported() {\n        return unsupported;\n    }\n\n    public int getFailures() {\n        return failures;\n    }\n\n    private void solveTypeDecl(ClassOrInterfaceDeclaration node) {\n        ResolvedTypeDeclaration typeDeclaration = JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).getTypeDeclaration(node);\n        if (typeDeclaration.isClass()) {\n            out.println(\"\\n[ Class \" + typeDeclaration.getQualifiedName() + \" ]\");\n            for (ResolvedReferenceType sc : typeDeclaration.asClass().getAllSuperClasses()) {\n                out.println(\"  superclass: \" + sc.getQualifiedName());\n            }\n            for (ResolvedReferenceType sc : typeDeclaration.asClass().getAllInterfaces()) {\n                out.println(\"  interface: \" + sc.getQualifiedName());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void solve(Node node) {\n        if (node instanceof ClassOrInterfaceDeclaration) {\n            solveTypeDecl((ClassOrInterfaceDeclaration) node);\n        } else if (node instanceof Expression) {\n            Node parentNode = demandParentNode(node);\n            if (parentNode instanceof ImportDeclaration ||\n                    parentNode instanceof Expression ||\n                    parentNode instanceof MethodDeclaration ||\n                    parentNode instanceof PackageDeclaration) {\n                // skip\n                return;\n            }\n            if (parentNode instanceof Statement ||\n                    parentNode instanceof VariableDeclarator ||\n                    parentNode instanceof SwitchEntry) {\n                try {\n                    ResolvedType ref = JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).getType(node);\n                    out.println(\"  Line \" + lineNr(node) + \") \" + node + \" ==> \" + ref.describe());\n                    successes++;\n                } catch (UnsupportedOperationException upe) {\n                    unsupported++;\n                    err.println(upe.getMessage());\n                    throw upe;\n                } catch (RuntimeException re) {\n                    failures++;\n                    err.println(re.getMessage());\n                    throw re;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void solveMethodCalls(Node node) {\n        if (node instanceof MethodCallExpr) {\n            out.println(\"  Line \" + lineNr(node) + \") \" + node + \" ==> \" + toString((MethodCallExpr) node));\n        }\n        for (Node child : node.getChildNodes()) {\n            solveMethodCalls(child);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String toString(MethodCallExpr node) {\n        try {\n            return toString(JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).solve(node));\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            if (verbose) {\n                System.err.println(\"Error resolving call at L\" + lineNr(node) + \": \" + node);\n                e.printStackTrace();\n            }\n            return \"ERROR\";\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String toString(SymbolReference<ResolvedMethodDeclaration> methodDeclarationSymbolReference) {\n        if (methodDeclarationSymbolReference.isSolved()) {\n            return methodDeclarationSymbolReference.getCorrespondingDeclaration().getQualifiedSignature();\n        } else {\n            return \"UNSOLVED\";\n        }\n    }\n\n    private List<Node> collectAllNodes(Node node) {\n        List<Node> nodes = new ArrayList<>();\n        node.walk(nodes::add);\n        nodes.sort(comparing(n -> n.getBegin().get()));\n        return nodes;\n    }\n\n    public void solve(Path path) throws IOException {\n        Files.walkFileTree(path, new SimpleFileVisitor<Path>() {\n            @Override\n            public FileVisitResult visitFile(Path file, BasicFileAttributes attrs) throws IOException {\n                if (file.toString().endsWith(\".java\")) {\n                    if (printFileName) {\n                        out.println(\"- parsing \" + file.toAbsolutePath());\n                    }\n                    CompilationUnit cu = parse(file);\n                    List<Node> nodes = collectAllNodes(cu);\n                    nodes.forEach(n -> solve(n));\n                }\n                return FileVisitResult.CONTINUE;\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    public void solveMethodCalls(Path path) throws IOException {\n        Files.walkFileTree(path, new SimpleFileVisitor<Path>() {\n            @Override\n            public FileVisitResult visitFile(Path file, BasicFileAttributes attrs) throws IOException {\n                if (file.toString().endsWith(\".java\")) {\n                    if (printFileName) {\n                        out.println(\"- parsing \" + file.toAbsolutePath());\n                    }\n                    CompilationUnit cu = parse(file);\n                    solveMethodCalls(cu);\n                }\n                return FileVisitResult.CONTINUE;\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    private int lineNr(Node node) {\n        return node.getRange().map(range -> range.begin.line).orElseThrow(IllegalStateException::new);\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "javaparser-symbol-solver-core/src/main/java/com/github/javaparser/symbolsolver/javaparser/Navigator.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2015-2016 Federico Tomassetti\n * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparser;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.CompilationUnit;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.*;\n\nimport java.util.Optional;\n\n/**\n * This class can be used to easily retrieve nodes from a JavaParser AST.\n *\n * Note that methods with the prefix `demand` indicate that if the search value is not found, an exception will be thrown.\n *\n * @author Federico Tomassetti\n */\npublic final class Navigator {\n\n    private Navigator() {\n        // prevent instantiation\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Returns the {@code (i+1)}'th constructor of the given type declaration, in textual order. The constructor that\n     * appears first has the index 0, the second one the index 1, and so on.\n     *\n     * @param td    The type declaration to search in. Note that only classes and enums have constructors.\n     * @param index The index of the desired constructor.\n     * @return The desired ConstructorDeclaration if it was found, else an exception is thrown.\n     */\n    public static ConstructorDeclaration demandConstructor(TypeDeclaration<?> td, int index) {\n        return NavigatorDeclarationLookup.demandConstructor(td, index);\n    }\n\n    public static VariableDeclarator demandField(ClassOrInterfaceDeclaration cd, String name) {\n        return NavigatorDeclarationLookup.demandField(cd, name);\n    }\n\n    public static MethodDeclaration demandMethod(TypeDeclaration<?> cd, String name) {\n        return NavigatorDeclarationLookup.demandMethod(cd, name);\n    }\n\n    public static ClassOrInterfaceDeclaration demandClass(CompilationUnit cu, String qualifiedName) {\n        return NavigatorTypeLookup.demandClass(cu, qualifiedName);\n    }\n\n    public static ClassOrInterfaceDeclaration demandClassOrInterface(CompilationUnit cu, String qualifiedName) {\n        return NavigatorTypeLookup.demandClassOrInterface(cu, qualifiedName);\n    }\n\n    public static EnumDeclaration demandEnum(CompilationUnit cu, String qualifiedName) {\n        return NavigatorTypeLookup.demandEnum(cu, qualifiedName);\n    }\n\n    public static ClassOrInterfaceDeclaration demandInterface(CompilationUnit cu, String qualifiedName) {\n        return NavigatorTypeLookup.demandInterface(cu, qualifiedName);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Looks among the type declared in the Compilation Unit for one having the specified name.\n     * The name can be qualified with respect to the compilation unit. For example, if the compilation\n     * unit is in package a.b; and it contains two top level classes named C and D, with class E being defined inside D\n     * then the qualifiedName that can be resolved are \"C\", \"D\", and \"D.E\".\n     */\n    public static Optional<TypeDeclaration<?>> findType(CompilationUnit cu, String qualifiedName) {\n        return NavigatorTypeLookup.findType(cu, qualifiedName);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Looks among the type declared in the TypeDeclaration for one having the specified name.\n     * The name can be qualified with respect to the TypeDeclaration. For example, if the class declaration defines\n     * class D and class D contains an internal class named E then the qualifiedName that can be resolved are \"D\", and\n     * \"D.E\".\n     */\n    public static Optional<TypeDeclaration<?>> findType(TypeDeclaration<?> td, String qualifiedName) {\n        return NavigatorTypeLookup.findType(td, qualifiedName);\n    }\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "javaparser-symbol-solver-core/src/main/java/com/github/javaparser/symbolsolver/javaparser/NavigatorNodeLookup.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2015-2016 Federico Tomassetti\n * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparser;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.Node;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.MethodDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.VariableDeclarator;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.MethodCallExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.NameExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.SimpleName;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.stmt.ReturnStmt;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.stmt.SwitchStmt;\n\nimport java.util.Optional;\n\n/**\n * Utility methods for searching and demanding nodes in a JavaParser AST.\n */\npublic final class NavigatorNodeLookup {\n\n    private NavigatorNodeLookup() {\n        // prevent instantiation\n    }\n\n    public static <N extends Node> N demandNodeOfGivenClass(Node node, Class<N> clazz) {\n        return node.findFirst(clazz).orElseThrow(IllegalArgumentException::new);\n    }\n\n    public static Node demandParentNode(Node node) {\n        return node.getParentNode().orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException(\"Parent not found, the node does not appear to be inserted in a correct AST\"));\n    }\n\n    public static ReturnStmt demandReturnStmt(MethodDeclaration method) {\n        return demandNodeOfGivenClass(method, ReturnStmt.class);\n    }\n\n    public static SwitchStmt demandSwitch(Node node) {\n        return findSwitchHelper(node).orElseThrow(IllegalArgumentException::new);\n    }\n\n    public static Optional<VariableDeclarator> demandVariableDeclaration(Node node, String name) {\n        return node.findFirst(VariableDeclarator.class, n -> n.getNameAsString().equals(name));\n    }\n\n    public static Optional<MethodCallExpr> findMethodCall(Node node, String methodName) {\n        return node.findFirst(MethodCallExpr.class, n -> n.getNameAsString().equals(methodName));\n    }\n\n    public static Optional<NameExpr> findNameExpression(Node node, String name) {\n        return node.findFirst(NameExpr.class, n -> n.getNameAsString().equals(name));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @deprecated Use {@link #demandNodeOfGivenClass(Node, Class)}\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public static <N extends Node> N findNodeOfGivenClass(Node node, Class<N> clazz) {\n        return demandNodeOfGivenClass(node, clazz);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @deprecated Use {@link #demandReturnStmt(MethodDeclaration)}\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public static ReturnStmt findReturnStmt(MethodDeclaration method) {\n        return demandReturnStmt(method);\n    }\n\n    public static Optional<SimpleName> findSimpleName(Node node, String name) {\n        return node.findFirst(SimpleName.class, n -> n.asString().equals(name));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @deprecated Use {@link #demandSwitch(Node)}\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public static SwitchStmt findSwitch(Node node) {\n        return demandSwitch(node);\n    }\n\n    private static Optional<SwitchStmt> findSwitchHelper(Node node) {\n        if (node instanceof SwitchStmt) {\n            return Optional.of((SwitchStmt) node);\n        }\n\n        return node.findFirst(SwitchStmt.class);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @deprecated Use {@link #demandParentNode(Node)}\n     */\n    @Deprecated\n    public static Node requireParentNode(Node node) {\n        return demandParentNode(node);\n    }\n}\n"
    },
