      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2015-2016 Federico Tomassetti\n * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.contexts;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.Node;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.*;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.nodeTypes.NodeWithOptionalScope;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.Context;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.MethodUsage;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.TypeSolver;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.UnsolvedSymbolException;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedMethodDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedReferenceTypeDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedTypeDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedTypeParameterDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedValueDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.SymbolReference;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.Value;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedReferenceType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.core.resolution.TypeVariableResolutionCapability;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.JavaParserFacade;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.JavaParserFactory;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.declarations.JavaParserPatternDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.declarations.JavaParserSymbolDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.reflectionmodel.ReflectionClassDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.resolution.SymbolDeclarator;\n\nimport java.util.*;\n\nimport static com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparser.Navigator.demandParentNode;\nimport static java.util.Collections.singletonList;\n\n/**\n * @author Federico Tomassetti\n */\npublic abstract class AbstractJavaParserContext<N extends Node> implements Context {\n\n    protected N wrappedNode;\n    protected TypeSolver typeSolver;\n\n    ///\n    /// Static methods\n    ///\n    \n    protected static boolean isQualifiedName(String name) {\n        return name.contains(\".\");\n    }\n\n    public static SymbolReference<ResolvedValueDeclaration> solveWith(SymbolDeclarator symbolDeclarator, String name) {\n        for (ResolvedValueDeclaration decl : symbolDeclarator.getSymbolDeclarations()) {\n            if (decl.getName().equals(name)) {\n                return SymbolReference.solved(decl);\n            }\n        }\n        return SymbolReference.unsolved();\n    }\n\n    ///\n    /// Constructors\n    ///\n\n    public AbstractJavaParserContext(N wrappedNode, TypeSolver typeSolver) {\n        if (wrappedNode == null) {\n            throw new NullPointerException();\n        }\n        this.wrappedNode = wrappedNode;\n        this.typeSolver = typeSolver;\n    }\n\n    ///\n    /// Public methods\n    ///\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean equals(Object o) {\n        if (this == o) return true;\n        if (o == null || getClass() != o.getClass()) return false;\n\n        AbstractJavaParserContext<?> that = (AbstractJavaParserContext<?>) o;\n\n        return wrappedNode != null ? wrappedNode.equals(that.wrappedNode) : that.wrappedNode == null;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public int hashCode() {\n        return wrappedNode == null ? 0 : wrappedNode.hashCode();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public final Optional<Context> getParent() {\n        Node parentNode = wrappedNode.getParentNode().orElse(null);\n\n        // TODO/FiXME: Document why the method call expression is treated differently.\n        if (parentNode instanceof MethodCallExpr) {\n            MethodCallExpr parentCall = (MethodCallExpr) parentNode;\n            // TODO: Can this be replaced with: boolean found = parentCall.getArguments().contains(wrappedNode);\n            boolean found = false;\n            for (Expression expression : parentCall.getArguments()) {\n                if (expression == wrappedNode) {\n                    found = true;\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n            if (found) {\n                Node notMethod = parentNode;\n                while (notMethod instanceof MethodCallExpr) {\n                    notMethod = demandParentNode(notMethod);\n                }\n                return Optional.of(JavaParserFactory.getContext(notMethod, typeSolver));\n            }\n        }\n        Node notMethodNode = parentNode;\n        // to avoid an infinite loop if parent scope is the same as wrapped node \n        while (notMethodNode instanceof MethodCallExpr || notMethodNode instanceof FieldAccessExpr\n                || (notMethodNode != null && notMethodNode.hasScope() && getScope(notMethodNode).equals(wrappedNode)) ) {\n            notMethodNode = notMethodNode.getParentNode().orElse(null);\n        }\n        if (notMethodNode == null) {\n            return Optional.empty();\n        }\n        Context parentContext = JavaParserFactory.getContext(notMethodNode, typeSolver);\n        return Optional.of(parentContext);\n    }\n    \n    // before to call this method verify the node has a scope \n    protected Node getScope(Node node) {\n        return (Node) ((NodeWithOptionalScope)node).getScope().get();\n    }\n\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<? extends ResolvedValueDeclaration> solveSymbolInParentContext(String name) {\n        Optional<Context> optionalParentContext = getParent();\n        if (!optionalParentContext.isPresent()) {\n            return SymbolReference.unsolved();\n        }\n\n        // First check if there are any pattern expressions available to this node.\n        Context parentContext = optionalParentContext.get();\n        if(parentContext instanceof BinaryExprContext || parentContext instanceof IfStatementContext) {\n            List<PatternExpr> patternExprs = parentContext.patternExprsExposedToChild(this.getWrappedNode());\n\n            Optional<PatternExpr> localResolutionResults = patternExprs\n                    .stream()\n                    .filter(vd -> vd.getNameAsString().equals(name))\n                    .findFirst();\n\n            if (localResolutionResults.isPresent()) {\n                if(patternExprs.size() == 1) {\n                    JavaParserPatternDeclaration decl = JavaParserSymbolDeclaration.patternVar(localResolutionResults.get(), typeSolver);\n                    return SymbolReference.solved(decl);\n                } else if(patternExprs.size() > 1) {\n                    throw new IllegalStateException(\"Unexpectedly more than one reference in scope\");\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Delegate solving to the parent context.\n        return parentContext.solveSymbol(name);\n    }\n\n    ///\n    /// Protected methods\n    ///\n\n    protected Optional<Value> solveWithAsValue(SymbolDeclarator symbolDeclarator, String name) {\n        return symbolDeclarator.getSymbolDeclarations().stream()\n                .filter(d -> d.getName().equals(name))\n                .map(Value::from)\n                .findFirst();\n    }\n\n    protected Collection<ResolvedReferenceTypeDeclaration> findTypeDeclarations(Optional<Expression> optScope) {\n        if (optScope.isPresent()) {\n            Expression scope = optScope.get();\n\n            // consider static methods\n            if (scope instanceof NameExpr) {\n                NameExpr scopeAsName = scope.asNameExpr();\n                SymbolReference<ResolvedTypeDeclaration> symbolReference = this.solveType(scopeAsName.getName().getId());\n                if (symbolReference.isSolved() && symbolReference.getCorrespondingDeclaration().isType()) {\n                    return singletonList(symbolReference.getCorrespondingDeclaration().asReferenceType());\n                }\n            }\n\n            ResolvedType typeOfScope;\n            try {\n                typeOfScope = JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).getType(scope);\n            } catch (Exception e) {\n                // If the scope corresponds to a type we should treat it differently\n                if (scope instanceof FieldAccessExpr) {\n                    FieldAccessExpr scopeName = (FieldAccessExpr) scope;\n                    if (this.solveType(scopeName.toString()).isSolved()) {\n                        return Collections.emptyList();\n                    }\n                }\n                throw new UnsolvedSymbolException(scope.toString(), wrappedNode.toString(), e);\n            }\n            if (typeOfScope.isWildcard()) {\n                if (typeOfScope.asWildcard().isExtends() || typeOfScope.asWildcard().isSuper()) {\n                    // TODO: Figure out if it is appropriate to remove the orElseThrow() -- if so, how...\n                    return singletonList(\n                            typeOfScope.asWildcard()\n                                    .getBoundedType()\n                                    .asReferenceType()\n                                    .getTypeDeclaration()\n                                    .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException(\"TypeDeclaration unexpectedly empty.\"))\n                    );\n                } else {\n                    return singletonList(new ReflectionClassDeclaration(Object.class, typeSolver).asReferenceType());\n                }\n            } else if (typeOfScope.isArray()) {\n                // method call on array are Object methods\n                return singletonList(new ReflectionClassDeclaration(Object.class, typeSolver).asReferenceType());\n            } else if (typeOfScope.isTypeVariable()) {\n                Collection<ResolvedReferenceTypeDeclaration> result = new ArrayList<>();\n                for (ResolvedTypeParameterDeclaration.Bound bound : typeOfScope.asTypeParameter().getBounds()) {\n                    // TODO: Figure out if it is appropriate to remove the orElseThrow() -- if so, how...\n                    result.add(\n                            bound.getType()\n                                    .asReferenceType()\n                                    .getTypeDeclaration()\n                                    .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException(\"TypeDeclaration unexpectedly empty.\"))\n                    );\n                }\n                return result;\n            } else if (typeOfScope.isConstraint()) {\n                // TODO: Figure out if it is appropriate to remove the orElseThrow() -- if so, how...\n                return singletonList(\n                        typeOfScope.asConstraintType()\n                                .getBound()\n                                .asReferenceType()\n                                .getTypeDeclaration()\n                                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException(\"TypeDeclaration unexpectedly empty.\"))\n                );\n            } else if (typeOfScope.isUnionType()) {\n                return typeOfScope.asUnionType().getCommonAncestor()\n                        .flatMap(ResolvedReferenceType::getTypeDeclaration)\n                        .map(Collections::singletonList)\n                        .orElseThrow(() -> new UnsolvedSymbolException(\"No common ancestor available for UnionType\" + typeOfScope.describe()));\n            }\n\n            // TODO: Figure out if it is appropriate to remove the orElseThrow() -- if so, how...\n            return singletonList(\n                    typeOfScope.asReferenceType()\n                            .getTypeDeclaration()\n                            .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException(\"TypeDeclaration unexpectedly empty.\"))\n            );\n        }\n\n        ResolvedType typeOfScope = JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).getTypeOfThisIn(wrappedNode);\n\n        // TODO: Figure out if it is appropriate to remove the orElseThrow() -- if so, how...\n        return singletonList(\n                typeOfScope.asReferenceType()\n                        .getTypeDeclaration()\n                        .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException(\"TypeDeclaration unexpectedly empty.\"))\n        );\n    }\n    \n    /**\n     * Similar to solveMethod but we return a MethodUsage.\n     * A MethodUsage corresponds to a MethodDeclaration plus the resolved type variables.\n     */\n    public Optional<MethodUsage> solveMethodAsUsage(String name, List<ResolvedType> argumentsTypes) {\n        SymbolReference<ResolvedMethodDeclaration> methodSolved = solveMethod(name, argumentsTypes, false);\n        if (methodSolved.isSolved()) {\n            ResolvedMethodDeclaration methodDeclaration = methodSolved.getCorrespondingDeclaration();\n            if (!(methodDeclaration instanceof TypeVariableResolutionCapability)) {\n                throw new UnsupportedOperationException(String.format(\n                        \"Resolved method declarations must implement %s.\",\n                        TypeVariableResolutionCapability.class.getName()\n                ));\n            }\n\n            MethodUsage methodUsage = ((TypeVariableResolutionCapability) methodDeclaration).resolveTypeVariables(this, argumentsTypes);\n            return Optional.of(methodUsage);\n        } else {\n            return Optional.empty();\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public N getWrappedNode() {\n        return wrappedNode;\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "javaparser-symbol-solver-core/src/main/java/com/github/javaparser/symbolsolver/javaparsermodel/contexts/FieldAccessContext.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2015-2016 Federico Tomassetti\n * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.contexts;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.Expression;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.FieldAccessExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.ThisExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.TypeSolver;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.*;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.SymbolReference;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.Value;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedPrimitiveType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedReferenceType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.JavaParserFacade;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.JavaParserFactory;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.resolution.SymbolSolver;\n\nimport java.util.Collection;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Optional;\n\nimport static com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparser.Navigator.demandParentNode;\n\n/**\n * @author Federico Tomassetti\n */\npublic class FieldAccessContext extends AbstractJavaParserContext<FieldAccessExpr> {\n\n    private static final String ARRAY_LENGTH_FIELD_NAME = \"length\";\n\n    public FieldAccessContext(FieldAccessExpr wrappedNode, TypeSolver typeSolver) {\n        super(wrappedNode, typeSolver);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<? extends ResolvedValueDeclaration> solveSymbol(String name) {\n        if (wrappedNode.getName().toString().equals(name)) {\n            if (wrappedNode.getScope() instanceof ThisExpr) {\n                ResolvedType typeOfThis = JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).getTypeOfThisIn(wrappedNode);\n                if(typeOfThis.asReferenceType().getTypeDeclaration().isPresent()) {\n                    return new SymbolSolver(typeSolver).solveSymbolInType(\n                            typeOfThis.asReferenceType().getTypeDeclaration().get(),\n                            name\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        return JavaParserFactory.getContext(demandParentNode(wrappedNode), typeSolver).solveSymbol(name);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<ResolvedTypeDeclaration> solveType(String name, List<ResolvedType> typeArguments) {\n        return JavaParserFactory.getContext(demandParentNode(wrappedNode), typeSolver).solveType(name, typeArguments);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<ResolvedMethodDeclaration> solveMethod(String name, List<ResolvedType> parameterTypes, boolean staticOnly) {\n        return JavaParserFactory.getContext(demandParentNode(wrappedNode), typeSolver).solveMethod(name, parameterTypes, false);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public Optional<Value> solveSymbolAsValue(String name) {\n        Expression scope = wrappedNode.getScope();\n        if (wrappedNode.getName().toString().equals(name)) {\n            ResolvedType typeOfScope = JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).getType(scope);\n            if (typeOfScope.isArray() && name.equals(ARRAY_LENGTH_FIELD_NAME)) {\n                return Optional.of(new Value(ResolvedPrimitiveType.INT, ARRAY_LENGTH_FIELD_NAME));\n            }\n            if (typeOfScope.isReferenceType()) {\n                return solveSymbolAsValue(name, typeOfScope.asReferenceType());\n            } else if (typeOfScope.isConstraint()) {\n                return solveSymbolAsValue(name, typeOfScope.asConstraintType().getBound().asReferenceType());\n            } else {\n                return Optional.empty();\n            }\n        } else {\n            return solveSymbolAsValueInParentContext(name);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /*\n     * Try to resolve the name parameter as a field of the reference type\n     */\n    private Optional<Value> solveSymbolAsValue(String name, ResolvedReferenceType type) {\n        Optional<ResolvedReferenceTypeDeclaration> optionalTypeDeclaration = type.getTypeDeclaration();\n        if (optionalTypeDeclaration.isPresent()) {\n            ResolvedReferenceTypeDeclaration typeDeclaration = optionalTypeDeclaration.get();\n            if (typeDeclaration.isEnum()) {\n                ResolvedEnumDeclaration enumDeclaration = (ResolvedEnumDeclaration) typeDeclaration;\n                if (enumDeclaration.hasEnumConstant(name)) {\n                    return Optional.of(new Value(enumDeclaration.getEnumConstant(name).getType(), name));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        Optional<ResolvedType> typeUsage = type.getFieldType(name);\n        return typeUsage.map(resolvedType -> new Value(resolvedType, name));\n    }\n\n    public SymbolReference<ResolvedValueDeclaration> solveField(String name) {\n        Collection<ResolvedReferenceTypeDeclaration> rrtds = findTypeDeclarations(Optional.of(wrappedNode.getScope()));\n        for (ResolvedReferenceTypeDeclaration rrtd : rrtds) {\n            if (rrtd.isEnum()) {\n                Optional<ResolvedEnumConstantDeclaration> enumConstant = rrtd.asEnum().getEnumConstants().stream().filter(c -> c.getName().equals(name)).findFirst();\n                if (enumConstant.isPresent()) {\n                    return SymbolReference.solved(enumConstant.get());\n                }\n            }\n            try {\n                return SymbolReference.solved(rrtd.getField(wrappedNode.getName().getId()));\n            } catch (Throwable t) {\n            }\n        }\n        return SymbolReference.unsolved();\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "javaparser-symbol-solver-core/src/main/java/com/github/javaparser/symbolsolver/javaparsermodel/contexts/ForEachStatementContext.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2015-2016 Federico Tomassetti\n * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.contexts;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.Node;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.VariableDeclarator;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.stmt.BlockStmt;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.stmt.ForEachStmt;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.TypeSolver;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedMethodDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedValueDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.SymbolReference;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.declarations.JavaParserSymbolDeclaration;\n\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport static com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparser.Navigator.demandParentNode;\n\npublic class ForEachStatementContext extends AbstractJavaParserContext<ForEachStmt> {\n\n    public ForEachStatementContext(ForEachStmt wrappedNode, TypeSolver typeSolver) {\n        super(wrappedNode, typeSolver);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<? extends ResolvedValueDeclaration> solveSymbol(String name) {\n        if (wrappedNode.getVariable().getVariables().size() != 1) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException();\n        }\n        VariableDeclarator variableDeclarator = wrappedNode.getVariable().getVariables().get(0);\n        if (variableDeclarator.getName().getId().equals(name)) {\n            return SymbolReference.solved(JavaParserSymbolDeclaration.localVar(variableDeclarator, typeSolver));\n        } else {\n            if (demandParentNode(wrappedNode) instanceof BlockStmt) {\n                return StatementContext.solveInBlock(name, typeSolver, wrappedNode);\n            } else {\n                return solveSymbolInParentContext(name);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<ResolvedMethodDeclaration> solveMethod(String name, List<ResolvedType> argumentsTypes, boolean staticOnly) {\n        // TODO: Document why staticOnly is forced to be false.\n        return solveMethodInParentContext(name, argumentsTypes, false);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public List<VariableDeclarator> localVariablesExposedToChild(Node child) {\n        if (child == wrappedNode.getBody()) {\n            return wrappedNode.getVariable().getVariables();\n        }\n        return Collections.emptyList();\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "javaparser-symbol-solver-core/src/main/java/com/github/javaparser/symbolsolver/javaparsermodel/contexts/ForStatementContext.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2015-2016 Federico Tomassetti\n * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.contexts;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.Node;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.VariableDeclarator;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.AssignExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.Expression;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.MethodCallExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.UnaryExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.VariableDeclarationExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.nodeTypes.NodeWithStatements;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.stmt.ForStmt;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.TypeSolver;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedMethodDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedValueDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.SymbolReference;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.declarations.JavaParserSymbolDeclaration;\n\nimport java.util.LinkedList;\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport static com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparser.Navigator.demandParentNode;\n\npublic class ForStatementContext extends AbstractJavaParserContext<ForStmt> {\n\n    public ForStatementContext(ForStmt wrappedNode, TypeSolver typeSolver) {\n        super(wrappedNode, typeSolver);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<? extends ResolvedValueDeclaration> solveSymbol(String name) {\n        for (Expression expression : wrappedNode.getInitialization()) {\n            if (expression instanceof VariableDeclarationExpr) {\n                VariableDeclarationExpr variableDeclarationExpr = (VariableDeclarationExpr) expression;\n                for (VariableDeclarator variableDeclarator : variableDeclarationExpr.getVariables()) {\n                    if (variableDeclarator.getName().getId().equals(name)) {\n                        return SymbolReference.solved(JavaParserSymbolDeclaration.localVar(variableDeclarator, typeSolver));\n                    }\n                }\n            } else if (!(expression instanceof AssignExpr || expression instanceof MethodCallExpr || expression instanceof UnaryExpr)) {\n                throw new UnsupportedOperationException(expression.getClass().getCanonicalName());\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (demandParentNode(wrappedNode) instanceof NodeWithStatements) {\n            return StatementContext.solveInBlock(name, typeSolver, wrappedNode);\n        } else {\n            return solveSymbolInParentContext(name);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<ResolvedMethodDeclaration> solveMethod(String name, List<ResolvedType> argumentsTypes, boolean staticOnly) {\n        // TODO: Document why staticOnly is forced to be false.\n        return solveMethodInParentContext(name, argumentsTypes, false);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public List<VariableDeclarator> localVariablesExposedToChild(Node child) {\n        List<VariableDeclarator> res = new LinkedList<>();\n        for (Expression expression : wrappedNode.getInitialization()) {\n            if (expression instanceof VariableDeclarationExpr) {\n                VariableDeclarationExpr variableDeclarationExpr = (VariableDeclarationExpr) expression;\n                res.addAll(variableDeclarationExpr.getVariables());\n            }\n        }\n        return res;\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "javaparser-symbol-solver-core/src/main/java/com/github/javaparser/symbolsolver/javaparsermodel/contexts/LambdaExprContext.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2015-2016 Federico Tomassetti\n * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.contexts;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.Node;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.NodeList;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.MethodDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.Parameter;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.VariableDeclarator;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.CastExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.Expression;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.LambdaExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.MethodCallExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.stmt.ReturnStmt;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.MethodUsage;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.TypeSolver;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedMethodDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedTypeParameterDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedValueDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.logic.FunctionalInterfaceLogic;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.SymbolReference;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.Value;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.typesystem.ReferenceTypeImpl;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedLambdaConstraintType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.JavaParserFacade;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.JavaParserFactory;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.logic.InferenceContext;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.reflectionmodel.MyObjectProvider;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.resolution.SymbolDeclarator;\n\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.HashMap;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Map;\nimport java.util.Optional;\n\nimport static com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparser.Navigator.demandParentNode;\n\n/**\n * @author Federico Tomassetti\n */\npublic class LambdaExprContext extends AbstractJavaParserContext<LambdaExpr> {\n\n    public LambdaExprContext(LambdaExpr wrappedNode, TypeSolver typeSolver) {\n        super(wrappedNode, typeSolver);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public Optional<Value> solveSymbolAsValue(String name) {\n        int index = -1;\n        for (Parameter parameter : wrappedNode.getParameters()) {\n            index++;\n            SymbolDeclarator sb = JavaParserFactory.getSymbolDeclarator(parameter, typeSolver);\n            for (ResolvedValueDeclaration decl : sb.getSymbolDeclarations()) {\n                if (decl.getName().equals(name)) {\n                    Node parentNode = demandParentNode(wrappedNode);\n                    if (parentNode instanceof MethodCallExpr) {\n                        MethodCallExpr methodCallExpr = (MethodCallExpr) parentNode;\n                        MethodUsage methodUsage = JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).solveMethodAsUsage(methodCallExpr);\n                        int i = pos(methodCallExpr, wrappedNode);\n                        ResolvedType lambdaType = methodUsage.getParamTypes().get(i);\n\n                        // Get the functional method in order for us to resolve it's type arguments properly\n                        Optional<MethodUsage> functionalMethodOpt = FunctionalInterfaceLogic.getFunctionalMethod(lambdaType);\n                        if (functionalMethodOpt.isPresent()){\n                            MethodUsage functionalMethod = functionalMethodOpt.get();\n                            InferenceContext inferenceContext = new InferenceContext(MyObjectProvider.INSTANCE);\n\n                            // Resolve each type variable of the lambda, and use this later to infer the type of each\n                            // implicit parameter\n                            lambdaType.asReferenceType().getTypeDeclaration().ifPresent(typeDeclaration -> {\n                                inferenceContext.addPair(\n                                        lambdaType,\n                                        new ReferenceTypeImpl(typeDeclaration)\n                                );\n                            });\n\n                            // Find the position of this lambda argument\n                            boolean found = false;\n                            int lambdaParamIndex;\n                            for (lambdaParamIndex = 0; lambdaParamIndex < wrappedNode.getParameters().size(); lambdaParamIndex++){\n                                if (wrappedNode.getParameter(lambdaParamIndex).getName().getIdentifier().equals(name)){\n                                    found = true;\n                                    break;\n                                }\n                            }\n                            if (!found) { return Optional.empty(); }\n\n                            // Now resolve the argument type using the inference context\n                            ResolvedType argType = inferenceContext.resolve(inferenceContext.addSingle(functionalMethod.getParamType(lambdaParamIndex)));\n\n                            ResolvedLambdaConstraintType conType;\n                            if (argType.isWildcard()){\n                                conType = ResolvedLambdaConstraintType.bound(argType.asWildcard().getBoundedType());\n                            } else {\n                                conType = ResolvedLambdaConstraintType.bound(argType);\n                            }\n                            Value value = new Value(conType, name);\n                            return Optional.of(value);\n                        } else{\n                            return Optional.empty();\n                        }\n                    } else if (parentNode instanceof VariableDeclarator) {\n                        VariableDeclarator variableDeclarator = (VariableDeclarator) parentNode;\n                        ResolvedType t = JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).convertToUsage(variableDeclarator.getType());\n                        Optional<MethodUsage> functionalMethod = FunctionalInterfaceLogic.getFunctionalMethod(t);\n                        if (functionalMethod.isPresent()) {\n                            ResolvedType lambdaType = functionalMethod.get().getParamType(index);\n\n                            // Replace parameter from declarator\n                            Map<ResolvedTypeParameterDeclaration, ResolvedType> inferredTypes = new HashMap<>();\n                            if (lambdaType.isReferenceType()) {\n                                for (com.github.javaparser.utils.Pair<ResolvedTypeParameterDeclaration, ResolvedType> entry : lambdaType.asReferenceType().getTypeParametersMap()) {\n                                    if (entry.b.isTypeVariable() && entry.b.asTypeParameter().declaredOnType()) {\n                                        ResolvedType ot = t.asReferenceType().typeParametersMap().getValue(entry.a);\n                                        lambdaType = lambdaType.replaceTypeVariables(entry.a, ot, inferredTypes);\n                                    }\n                                }\n                            } else if (lambdaType.isTypeVariable() && lambdaType.asTypeParameter().declaredOnType()) {\n                                lambdaType = t.asReferenceType().typeParametersMap().getValue(lambdaType.asTypeParameter());\n                            }\n\n                            Value value = new Value(lambdaType, name);\n                            return Optional.of(value);\n                        } else {\n                            throw new UnsupportedOperationException();\n                        }\n                    } else if (parentNode instanceof ReturnStmt) {\n                        ReturnStmt returnStmt = (ReturnStmt) parentNode;\n                        Optional<MethodDeclaration> optDeclaration = returnStmt.findAncestor(MethodDeclaration.class);\n                        if (optDeclaration.isPresent()) {\n                            ResolvedType t = JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).convertToUsage(optDeclaration.get().asMethodDeclaration().getType());\n                            Optional<MethodUsage> functionalMethod = FunctionalInterfaceLogic.getFunctionalMethod(t);\n\n                            if (functionalMethod.isPresent()) {\n                                ResolvedType lambdaType = functionalMethod.get().getParamType(index);\n\n                                // Replace parameter from declarator\n                                Map<ResolvedTypeParameterDeclaration, ResolvedType> inferredTypes = new HashMap<>();\n                                if (lambdaType.isReferenceType()) {\n                                    for (com.github.javaparser.utils.Pair<ResolvedTypeParameterDeclaration, ResolvedType> entry : lambdaType.asReferenceType().getTypeParametersMap()) {\n                                        if (entry.b.isTypeVariable() && entry.b.asTypeParameter().declaredOnType()) {\n                                            ResolvedType ot = t.asReferenceType().typeParametersMap().getValue(entry.a);\n                                            lambdaType = lambdaType.replaceTypeVariables(entry.a, ot, inferredTypes);\n                                        }\n                                    }\n                                } else if (lambdaType.isTypeVariable() && lambdaType.asTypeParameter().declaredOnType()) {\n                                    lambdaType = t.asReferenceType().typeParametersMap().getValue(lambdaType.asTypeParameter());\n                                }\n\n                                Value value = new Value(lambdaType, name);\n                                return Optional.of(value);\n                            } else {\n                                throw new UnsupportedOperationException();\n                            }\n                        }\n                    } else if (parentNode instanceof CastExpr) {\n                        CastExpr castExpr = (CastExpr) parentNode;\n                        ResolvedType t = JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).convertToUsage(castExpr.getType());\n                        Optional<MethodUsage> functionalMethod = FunctionalInterfaceLogic.getFunctionalMethod(t);\n\n                        if (functionalMethod.isPresent()) {\n                            ResolvedType lambdaType = functionalMethod.get().getParamType(index);\n\n                            // Replace parameter from declarator\n                            Map<ResolvedTypeParameterDeclaration, ResolvedType> inferredTypes = new HashMap<>();\n                            if (lambdaType.isReferenceType()) {\n                                for (com.github.javaparser.utils.Pair<ResolvedTypeParameterDeclaration, ResolvedType> entry : lambdaType.asReferenceType().getTypeParametersMap()) {\n                                    if (entry.b.isTypeVariable() && entry.b.asTypeParameter().declaredOnType()) {\n                                        ResolvedType ot = t.asReferenceType().typeParametersMap().getValue(entry.a);\n                                        lambdaType = lambdaType.replaceTypeVariables(entry.a, ot, inferredTypes);\n                                    }\n                                }\n                            } else if (lambdaType.isTypeVariable() && lambdaType.asTypeParameter().declaredOnType()) {\n                                lambdaType = t.asReferenceType().typeParametersMap().getValue(lambdaType.asTypeParameter());\n                            }\n\n                            Value value = new Value(lambdaType, name);\n                            return Optional.of(value);\n                        } else {\n                            throw new UnsupportedOperationException();\n                        }\n                    } else {\n                        throw new UnsupportedOperationException();\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // if nothing is found we should ask the parent context\n        return solveSymbolAsValueInParentContext(name);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<? extends ResolvedValueDeclaration> solveSymbol(String name) {\n        for (Parameter parameter : wrappedNode.getParameters()) {\n            SymbolDeclarator sb = JavaParserFactory.getSymbolDeclarator(parameter, typeSolver);\n            SymbolReference<ResolvedValueDeclaration> symbolReference = solveWith(sb, name);\n            if (symbolReference.isSolved()) {\n                return symbolReference;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // if nothing is found we should ask the parent context\n        return solveSymbolInParentContext(name);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<ResolvedMethodDeclaration> solveMethod(String name, List<ResolvedType> argumentsTypes, boolean staticOnly) {\n        // TODO: Document why staticOnly is forced to be false.\n        return solveMethodInParentContext(name, argumentsTypes, false);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public List<Parameter> parametersExposedToChild(Node child) {\n        // TODO/FIXME: Presumably the parameters must be exposed to all children and their descendants, not just the direct child?\n        if (child == wrappedNode.getBody()) {\n            return wrappedNode.getParameters();\n        }\n        return Collections.emptyList();\n    }\n\n    ///\n    /// Protected methods\n    ///\n\n    protected final Optional<Value> solveWithAsValue(SymbolDeclarator symbolDeclarator, String name) {\n        for (ResolvedValueDeclaration decl : symbolDeclarator.getSymbolDeclarations()) {\n            if (decl.getName().equals(name)) {\n\n                throw new UnsupportedOperationException();\n            }\n        }\n        return Optional.empty();\n    }\n\n    ///\n    /// Private methods\n    ///\n\n    private int pos(MethodCallExpr callExpr, Expression param) {\n        int i = 0;\n        for (Expression p : callExpr.getArguments()) {\n            if (p == param) {\n                return i;\n            }\n            i++;\n        }\n        throw new IllegalArgumentException();\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2015-2016 Federico Tomassetti\n * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.contexts;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.MethodDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.VariableDeclarator;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.Expression;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.MethodCallExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.MethodReferenceExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.stmt.ReturnStmt;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.MethodUsage;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.TypeSolver;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedMethodDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedReferenceTypeDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedTypeParameterDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.logic.FunctionalInterfaceLogic;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.SymbolReference;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.typesystem.ReferenceTypeImpl;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedLambdaConstraintType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.JavaParserFacade;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.logic.InferenceContext;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.reflectionmodel.MyObjectProvider;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.resolution.MethodResolutionLogic;\n\nimport java.util.*;\n\nimport static com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparser.Navigator.demandParentNode;\n\npublic class MethodReferenceExprContext extends AbstractJavaParserContext<MethodReferenceExpr> {\n\n    ///\n    /// Constructors\n    ///\n\n    public MethodReferenceExprContext(MethodReferenceExpr wrappedNode, TypeSolver typeSolver) {\n        super(wrappedNode, typeSolver);\n    }\n\n    ///\n    /// Public methods\n    ///\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<ResolvedMethodDeclaration> solveMethod(String name, List<ResolvedType> argumentsTypes, boolean staticOnly) {\n        if (\"new\".equals(name)) {\n            throw new UnsupportedOperationException(\"Constructor calls not yet resolvable\");\n        }\n\n        argumentsTypes.addAll(inferArgumentTypes());\n\n        Collection<ResolvedReferenceTypeDeclaration> rrtds = findTypeDeclarations(Optional.of(wrappedNode.getScope()));\n\n        if (rrtds.isEmpty()) {\n            // if the bounds of a type parameter are empty, then the bound is implicitly \"extends Object\"\n            // we don't make this _ex_plicit in the data representation because that would affect codegen\n            // and make everything generate like <T extends Object> instead of <T>\n            // https://github.com/javaparser/javaparser/issues/2044\n            rrtds = Collections.singleton(typeSolver.getSolvedJavaLangObject());\n        }\n\n        for (ResolvedReferenceTypeDeclaration rrtd : rrtds) {\n            SymbolReference<ResolvedMethodDeclaration> firstResAttempt = MethodResolutionLogic.solveMethodInType(rrtd, name, argumentsTypes, false);\n            if (firstResAttempt.isSolved()) {\n                return firstResAttempt;\n            } else {\n                // If has not already been solved above then will be solved here if single argument type same as\n                // (or subclass of) rrtd, as call is actually performed on the argument itself with zero params\n                SymbolReference<ResolvedMethodDeclaration> secondResAttempt = MethodResolutionLogic.solveMethodInType(rrtd, name, Collections.emptyList(), false);\n                if (secondResAttempt.isSolved()) {\n                    return secondResAttempt;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        return SymbolReference.unsolved();\n    }\n\n    ///\n    /// Private methods\n    ///\n\n    private List<ResolvedType> inferArgumentTypes() {\n        if (demandParentNode(wrappedNode) instanceof MethodCallExpr) {\n            MethodCallExpr methodCallExpr = (MethodCallExpr) demandParentNode(wrappedNode);\n            MethodUsage methodUsage = JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).solveMethodAsUsage(methodCallExpr);\n            int pos = pos(methodCallExpr, wrappedNode);\n            ResolvedType lambdaType = methodUsage.getParamTypes().get(pos);\n\n            // Get the functional method in order for us to resolve it's type arguments properly\n            Optional<MethodUsage> functionalMethodOpt = FunctionalInterfaceLogic.getFunctionalMethod(lambdaType);\n            if (functionalMethodOpt.isPresent()) {\n                MethodUsage functionalMethod = functionalMethodOpt.get();\n\n                List<ResolvedType> resolvedTypes = new ArrayList<>();\n\n                for (ResolvedType type : functionalMethod.getParamTypes()) {\n                    InferenceContext inferenceContext = new InferenceContext(MyObjectProvider.INSTANCE);\n\n                    // Resolve each type variable of the lambda, and use this later to infer the type of each\n                    // implicit parameter\n                    inferenceContext.addPair(new ReferenceTypeImpl(functionalMethod.declaringType()), lambdaType);\n\n                    // Now resolve the argument type using the inference context\n                    ResolvedType argType = inferenceContext.resolve(inferenceContext.addSingle(type));\n\n                    ResolvedLambdaConstraintType conType;\n                    if (argType.isWildcard()){\n                        conType = ResolvedLambdaConstraintType.bound(argType.asWildcard().getBoundedType());\n                    } else {\n                        conType = ResolvedLambdaConstraintType.bound(argType);\n                    }\n\n                    resolvedTypes.add(conType);\n                }\n\n                return resolvedTypes;\n            } else {\n                throw new UnsupportedOperationException();\n            }\n        } else if (demandParentNode(wrappedNode) instanceof VariableDeclarator) {\n            VariableDeclarator variableDeclarator = (VariableDeclarator) demandParentNode(wrappedNode);\n            ResolvedType t = JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).convertToUsage(variableDeclarator.getType());\n            Optional<MethodUsage> functionalMethod = FunctionalInterfaceLogic.getFunctionalMethod(t);\n            if (functionalMethod.isPresent()) {\n                List<ResolvedType> resolvedTypes = new ArrayList<>();\n                for (ResolvedType lambdaType : functionalMethod.get().getParamTypes()) {\n                    // Replace parameter from declarator\n                    Map<ResolvedTypeParameterDeclaration, ResolvedType> inferredTypes = new HashMap<>();\n                    if (lambdaType.isReferenceType()) {\n                        for (com.github.javaparser.utils.Pair<ResolvedTypeParameterDeclaration, ResolvedType> entry : lambdaType.asReferenceType().getTypeParametersMap()) {\n                            if (entry.b.isTypeVariable() && entry.b.asTypeParameter().declaredOnType()) {\n                                ResolvedType ot = t.asReferenceType().typeParametersMap().getValue(entry.a);\n                                lambdaType = lambdaType.replaceTypeVariables(entry.a, ot, inferredTypes);\n                            }\n                        }\n                    } else if (lambdaType.isTypeVariable() && lambdaType.asTypeParameter().declaredOnType()) {\n                        lambdaType = t.asReferenceType().typeParametersMap().getValue(lambdaType.asTypeParameter());\n                    }\n                    resolvedTypes.add(lambdaType);\n                }\n\n                return resolvedTypes;\n            } else {\n                throw new UnsupportedOperationException();\n            }\n        } else if (demandParentNode(wrappedNode) instanceof ReturnStmt) {\n            ReturnStmt returnStmt = (ReturnStmt) demandParentNode(wrappedNode);\n            Optional<MethodDeclaration> optDeclaration = returnStmt.findAncestor(MethodDeclaration.class);\n            if (optDeclaration.isPresent()) {\n                ResolvedType t = JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).convertToUsage(optDeclaration.get().asMethodDeclaration().getType());\n                Optional<MethodUsage> functionalMethod = FunctionalInterfaceLogic.getFunctionalMethod(t);\n                if (functionalMethod.isPresent()) {\n                    List<ResolvedType> resolvedTypes = new ArrayList<>();\n                    for (ResolvedType lambdaType : functionalMethod.get().getParamTypes()) {\n                        // Replace parameter from declarator\n                        Map<ResolvedTypeParameterDeclaration, ResolvedType> inferredTypes = new HashMap<>();\n                        if (lambdaType.isReferenceType()) {\n                            for (com.github.javaparser.utils.Pair<ResolvedTypeParameterDeclaration, ResolvedType> entry : lambdaType.asReferenceType().getTypeParametersMap()) {\n                                if (entry.b.isTypeVariable() && entry.b.asTypeParameter().declaredOnType()) {\n                                    ResolvedType ot = t.asReferenceType().typeParametersMap().getValue(entry.a);\n                                    lambdaType = lambdaType.replaceTypeVariables(entry.a, ot, inferredTypes);\n                                }\n                            }\n                        } else if (lambdaType.isTypeVariable() && lambdaType.asTypeParameter().declaredOnType()) {\n                            lambdaType = t.asReferenceType().typeParametersMap().getValue(lambdaType.asTypeParameter());\n                        }\n                        resolvedTypes.add(lambdaType);\n                    }\n\n                    return resolvedTypes;\n                } else {\n                    throw new UnsupportedOperationException();\n                }\n            }\n            throw new UnsupportedOperationException();\n        } else {\n            throw new UnsupportedOperationException();\n        }\n    }\n\n    private int pos(MethodCallExpr callExpr, Expression param) {\n        int i = 0;\n        for (Expression p : callExpr.getArguments()) {\n            if (p == param) {\n                return i;\n            }\n            i++;\n        }\n        throw new IllegalArgumentException();\n    }\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "javaparser-symbol-solver-core/src/main/java/com/github/javaparser/symbolsolver/javaparsermodel/contexts/ObjectCreationContext.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2015-2016 Federico Tomassetti\n * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.contexts;\n\nimport static com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparser.Navigator.demandParentNode;\n\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.Node;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.Expression;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.ObjectCreationExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.TypeSolver;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedMethodDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedReferenceTypeDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedTypeDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedValueDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.SymbolReference;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.JavaParserFacade;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.JavaParserFactory;\n\n/**\n * @author Federico Tomassetti\n */\npublic class ObjectCreationContext extends AbstractJavaParserContext<ObjectCreationExpr> {\n\n    public ObjectCreationContext(ObjectCreationExpr wrappedNode, TypeSolver typeSolver) {\n        super(wrappedNode, typeSolver);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<ResolvedTypeDeclaration> solveType(String name, List<ResolvedType> typeArguments) {\n        if (wrappedNode.hasScope()) {\n            Expression scope = wrappedNode.getScope().get();\n            ResolvedType scopeType = JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).getType(scope);\n            // Be careful, the scope can be an object creation expression like <code>new inner().new Other()</code>\n            if (scopeType.isReferenceType() && scopeType.asReferenceType().getTypeDeclaration().isPresent()) {\n                ResolvedReferenceTypeDeclaration scopeTypeDeclaration = scopeType.asReferenceType().getTypeDeclaration().get();\n                for (ResolvedTypeDeclaration it : scopeTypeDeclaration.internalTypes()) {\n                    if (it.getName().equals(name)) {\n                        return SymbolReference.solved(it);\n                    }\n                }\n            } \n        }\n        // find first parent node that is not an object creation expression to avoid stack overflow errors, see #1711\n        Node parentNode = demandParentNode(wrappedNode);\n        while (parentNode instanceof ObjectCreationExpr) {\n            parentNode = demandParentNode(parentNode);\n        }\n        return JavaParserFactory.getContext(parentNode, typeSolver).solveType(name, typeArguments);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<? extends ResolvedValueDeclaration> solveSymbol(String name) {\n        return JavaParserFactory.getContext(demandParentNode(wrappedNode), typeSolver).solveSymbol(name);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<ResolvedMethodDeclaration> solveMethod(String name, List<ResolvedType> argumentsTypes, boolean staticOnly) {\n        return JavaParserFactory.getContext(demandParentNode(wrappedNode), typeSolver).solveMethod(name, argumentsTypes, false);\n    }\n\n}\n"
    },

      "path": "javaparser-symbol-solver-core/src/main/java/com/github/javaparser/symbolsolver/javaparsermodel/contexts/SwitchEntryContext.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2015-2016 Federico Tomassetti\n * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.contexts;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.stmt.Statement;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.stmt.SwitchEntry;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.stmt.SwitchStmt;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.TypeSolver;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedMethodDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedReferenceTypeDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedValueDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.SymbolReference;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.typesystem.ReferenceTypeImpl;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.JavaParserFacade;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.JavaParserFactory;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.resolution.SymbolDeclarator;\n\nimport java.util.List;\n\nimport static com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparser.Navigator.demandParentNode;\n\n/**\n * @author Federico Tomassetti\n */\npublic class SwitchEntryContext extends AbstractJavaParserContext<SwitchEntry> {\n\n    public SwitchEntryContext(SwitchEntry wrappedNode, TypeSolver typeSolver) {\n        super(wrappedNode, typeSolver);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<? extends ResolvedValueDeclaration> solveSymbol(String name) {\n        SwitchStmt switchStmt = (SwitchStmt) demandParentNode(wrappedNode);\n        ResolvedType type = JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).getType(switchStmt.getSelector());\n        if (type.isReferenceType() && type.asReferenceType().getTypeDeclaration().isPresent()) {\n            ResolvedReferenceTypeDeclaration typeDeclaration = type.asReferenceType().getTypeDeclaration().get();\n            if (typeDeclaration.isEnum()) {\n                if (type instanceof ReferenceTypeImpl) {\n                    ReferenceTypeImpl referenceType = (ReferenceTypeImpl) type;\n                    if(referenceType.getTypeDeclaration().isPresent()) {\n                        ResolvedReferenceTypeDeclaration typeUsageTypeDeclaration = referenceType.getTypeDeclaration().get();\n                        if (typeUsageTypeDeclaration.asEnum().hasEnumConstant(name)) {\n                            return SymbolReference.solved(typeUsageTypeDeclaration.asEnum().getEnumConstant(name));\n                        }\n                        if (typeUsageTypeDeclaration.hasField(name)) {\n                            return SymbolReference.solved(typeUsageTypeDeclaration.getField(name));\n                        }\n                    } else {\n                        // Consider IllegalStateException or similar?\n                    }\n                } else {\n                    throw new UnsupportedOperationException();\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // look for declaration in this and previous switch entry statements\n        for (SwitchEntry seStmt : switchStmt.getEntries()) {\n            for (Statement stmt : seStmt.getStatements()) {\n                SymbolDeclarator symbolDeclarator = JavaParserFactory.getSymbolDeclarator(stmt, typeSolver);\n                SymbolReference<? extends ResolvedValueDeclaration> symbolReference = solveWith(symbolDeclarator, name);\n                if (symbolReference.isSolved()) {\n                    return symbolReference;\n                }\n            }\n            // once we reach this switch entry statement, stop: we do not want to look in later switch entry statements\n            if (seStmt == wrappedNode) {\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        return solveSymbolInParentContext(name);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<ResolvedMethodDeclaration> solveMethod(String name, List<ResolvedType> argumentsTypes, boolean staticOnly) {\n        // TODO: Document why staticOnly is forced to be false.\n        return solveMethodInParentContext(name, argumentsTypes, false);\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "javaparser-symbol-solver-core/src/main/java/com/github/javaparser/symbolsolver/javaparsermodel/contexts/TryWithResourceContext.java",
      "exists": "true",
      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2015-2016 Federico Tomassetti\n * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.contexts;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.Node;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.NodeList;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.VariableDeclarator;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.Expression;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.expr.VariableDeclarationExpr;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.stmt.BlockStmt;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.stmt.TryStmt;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.TypeSolver;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedMethodDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedValueDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.SymbolReference;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.Value;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.declarations.JavaParserSymbolDeclaration;\n\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.LinkedList;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Optional;\nimport java.util.stream.Collectors;\n\nimport static com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparser.Navigator.demandParentNode;\n\npublic class TryWithResourceContext extends AbstractJavaParserContext<TryStmt> {\n\n    public TryWithResourceContext(TryStmt wrappedNode, TypeSolver typeSolver) {\n        super(wrappedNode, typeSolver);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public Optional<Value> solveSymbolAsValue(String name) {\n        for (Expression expr : wrappedNode.getResources()) {\n            if (expr instanceof VariableDeclarationExpr) {\n                for (VariableDeclarator v : ((VariableDeclarationExpr)expr).getVariables()) {\n                    if (v.getName().getIdentifier().equals(name)) {\n                        ResolvedValueDeclaration decl = JavaParserSymbolDeclaration.localVar(v, typeSolver);\n                        return Optional.of(Value.from(decl));\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (demandParentNode(wrappedNode) instanceof BlockStmt) {\n            return StatementContext.solveInBlockAsValue(name, typeSolver, wrappedNode);\n        } else {\n            return solveSymbolAsValueInParentContext(name);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<? extends ResolvedValueDeclaration> solveSymbol(String name) {\n        for (Expression expr : wrappedNode.getResources()) {\n            if (expr instanceof VariableDeclarationExpr) {\n                for (VariableDeclarator v : ((VariableDeclarationExpr)expr).getVariables()) {\n                    if (v.getName().getIdentifier().equals(name)) {\n                        return SymbolReference.solved(JavaParserSymbolDeclaration.localVar(v, typeSolver));\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (demandParentNode(wrappedNode) instanceof BlockStmt) {\n            return StatementContext.solveInBlock(name, typeSolver, wrappedNode);\n        } else {\n            return solveSymbolInParentContext(name);\n        }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public SymbolReference<ResolvedMethodDeclaration> solveMethod(String name, List<ResolvedType> argumentsTypes, boolean staticOnly) {\n        // TODO: Document why staticOnly is forced to be false.\n        return solveMethodInParentContext(name, argumentsTypes, false);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public List<VariableDeclarator> localVariablesExposedToChild(Node child) {\n        NodeList<Expression> resources = wrappedNode.getResources();\n        for (int i=0;i<resources.size();i++) {\n            if (child == resources.get(i)) {\n                return resources.subList(0, i).stream()\n                        .map(e -> e instanceof VariableDeclarationExpr ? ((VariableDeclarationExpr) e).getVariables()\n                                : Collections.<VariableDeclarator>emptyList())\n                        .flatMap(List::stream)\n                        .collect(Collectors.toList());\n            }\n        }\n        if (child == wrappedNode.getTryBlock()) {\n            List<VariableDeclarator> res = new LinkedList<>();\n            for (Expression expr : resources) {\n                if (expr instanceof VariableDeclarationExpr) {\n                    res.addAll(((VariableDeclarationExpr)expr).getVariables());\n                }\n            }\n            return res;\n        }\n        return Collections.emptyList();\n    }\n}\n"
    },

See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.declarations;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.EnumDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.TypeSolver;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedEnumConstantDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.model.typesystem.ReferenceTypeImpl;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedType;\n\nimport static com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparser.Navigator.demandParentNode;\n\n/**\n * @author Federico Tomassetti\n */\npublic class JavaParserEnumConstantDeclaration implements ResolvedEnumConstantDeclaration {\n\n    private TypeSolver typeSolver;\n    private com.github.javaparser.ast.body.EnumConstantDeclaration wrappedNode;\n\n    public JavaParserEnumConstantDeclaration(com.github.javaparser.ast.body.EnumConstantDeclaration wrappedNode, TypeSolver typeSolver) {\n        this.wrappedNode = wrappedNode;\n        this.typeSolver = typeSolver;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public ResolvedType getType() {\n        return new ReferenceTypeImpl(new JavaParserEnumDeclaration((EnumDeclaration) demandParentNode(wrappedNode), typeSolver));\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String getName() {\n        return wrappedNode.getName().getId();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Returns the JavaParser node associated with this JavaParserEnumConstantDeclaration.\n     *\n     * @return A visitable JavaParser node wrapped by this object.\n     */\n    public com.github.javaparser.ast.body.EnumConstantDeclaration getWrappedNode() {\n        return wrappedNode;\n    }\n\n}\n"

      "content": "/*\n * Copyright (C) 2015-2016 Federico Tomassetti\n * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 The JavaParser Team.\n *\n * This file is part of JavaParser.\n *\n * JavaParser can be used either under the terms of\n * a) the GNU Lesser General Public License as published by\n *     the Free Software Foundation, either version 3 of the License, or\n *     (at your option) any later version.\n * b) the terms of the Apache License\n *\n * You should have received a copy of both licenses in LICENCE.LGPL and\n * LICENCE.APACHE. Please refer to those files for details.\n *\n * JavaParser is distributed in the hope that it will be useful,\n * but WITHOUT ANY WARRANTY; without even the implied warranty of\n * MERCHANTABILITY or FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.  See the\n * GNU Lesser General Public License for more details.\n */\n\npackage com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.declarations;\n\nimport static com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparser.Navigator.demandParentNode;\n\nimport java.util.Optional;\n\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.AccessSpecifier;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.Modifier;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.FieldDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.TypeDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.ast.body.VariableDeclarator;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.TypeSolver;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.AssociableToAST;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedFieldDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.declarations.ResolvedTypeDeclaration;\nimport com.github.javaparser.resolution.types.ResolvedType;\nimport com.github.javaparser.symbolsolver.javaparsermodel.JavaParserFacade;\n\n/**\n * @author Federico Tomassetti\n */\npublic class JavaParserFieldDeclaration implements ResolvedFieldDeclaration, AssociableToAST<FieldDeclaration> {\n\n    private VariableDeclarator variableDeclarator;\n    private com.github.javaparser.ast.body.FieldDeclaration wrappedNode;\n    private TypeSolver typeSolver;\n\n    public JavaParserFieldDeclaration(VariableDeclarator variableDeclarator, TypeSolver typeSolver) {\n        if (typeSolver == null) {\n            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"typeSolver should not be null\");\n        }\n        this.variableDeclarator = variableDeclarator;\n        this.typeSolver = typeSolver;\n        if (!(demandParentNode(variableDeclarator) instanceof com.github.javaparser.ast.body.FieldDeclaration)) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(demandParentNode(variableDeclarator).getClass().getCanonicalName());\n        }\n        this.wrappedNode = (com.github.javaparser.ast.body.FieldDeclaration) demandParentNode(variableDeclarator);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public ResolvedType getType() {\n        return JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).convert(variableDeclarator.getType(), wrappedNode);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String getName() {\n        return variableDeclarator.getName().getId();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean isStatic() {\n        return wrappedNode.hasModifier(Modifier.Keyword.STATIC);\n    }\n    \n    @Override\n    public boolean isVolatile() {\n        return wrappedNode.hasModifier(Modifier.Keyword.VOLATILE);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public boolean isField() {\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Returns the JavaParser node associated with this JavaParserFieldDeclaration.\n     *\n     * @return A visitable JavaParser node wrapped by this object.\n     */\n    public com.github.javaparser.ast.body.FieldDeclaration getWrappedNode() {\n        return wrappedNode;\n    }\n\n    public VariableDeclarator getVariableDeclarator() {\n        return variableDeclarator;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String toString() {\n        return \"JavaParserFieldDeclaration{\" + getName() + \"}\";\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public AccessSpecifier accessSpecifier() {\n        return wrappedNode.getAccessSpecifier();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public ResolvedTypeDeclaration declaringType() {\n        Optional<TypeDeclaration> typeDeclaration = wrappedNode.findAncestor(TypeDeclaration.class);\n        if (typeDeclaration.isPresent()) {\n            return JavaParserFacade.get(typeSolver).getTypeDeclaration(typeDeclaration.get());\n        }\n        throw new IllegalStateException();\n    }\n    \n    @Override\n    public Optional<FieldDeclaration> toAst() {\n        return Optional.ofNullable(wrappedNode);\n    }\n}\n"
    },
